Problem: Write a function to find squares of individual elements in a list using lambda function.
Signature: square_nums(seq) -> seq
t 1
gate loops
task mbpp_8__square_nums(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) == len(s)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(s)) . r[i] == s[i] * s[i]
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant len(r) == i
    invariant forall j in [0, i) . r[j] == s[j] * s[j]
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    r := r + [s[i] * s[i]];
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

Problem: Write a function to multiply two integers without using the * operator in python.
Signature: multiply_int(int, int) -> int
t 1
gate loops
task mbpp_127__multiply_int(a: int, b: int) returns (r: int)
  requires a >= 0
  requires b >= 0
  ensures r == a * b
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < b
    invariant r == a * i
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= b
    decreases b - i
  {
    r := r + a;
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

Problem: Write a function to remove everything except alphanumeric characters from a string.
Signature: remove_splchar(seq) -> seq
t 1
task mbpp_173__remove_splchar(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(r)) . r[i] >= 48 and r[i] <= 57 or r[i] >= 65 and r[i] <= 90 or r[i] >= 97 and r[i] <= 122
  ensures len(r) <= len(s)
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant len(r) <= i
    invariant forall j in [0, len(r)) . r[j] >= 48 and r[j] <= 57 or r[j] >= 65 and r[j] <= 90 or r[j] >= 97 and r[j] <= 122
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] >= 48 and s[i] <= 57 or s[i] >= 65 and s[i] <= 90 or s[i] >= 97 and s[i] <= 122 {
      r := r + [s[i]];
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

Problem: Write a python function to count the number of integral co-ordinates that lie inside a square.
Signature: count_Intgral_Points(int, int, int, int) -> int
t 0
task mbpp_190__count_Intgral_Points(x1: int, y1: int, x2: int, y2: int) returns (r: int)
  requires x1 <= x2
  requires y1 <= y2
  ensures r == (x2 - x1 - 1) * (y2 - y1 - 1) or x1 == x2 and y1 == y2 and r == 1
{
  if x1 == x2 and y1 == y2 {
    r := 1;
  } else {
    r := (x2 - x1 - 1) * (y2 - y1 - 1);
  }
}


Problem: Write a python function to find the first position of an element in a sorted array.
Signature: first(seq, int, int) -> int
t 1
gate loops
task mbpp_195__first(arr: seq, x: int, n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  requires len(arr) >= n
  ensures r == -1 or r >= 0 and r < n and arr[r] == x
  ensures r == -1 ==> (forall i in [0, n) . arr[i] != x)
  ensures r != -1 ==> (forall i in [0, r) . arr[i] != x)
{
  r := -1;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n and r == -1
    invariant r == -1 ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j < n ==> arr[j] != x)
    invariant r != -1 ==> r >= 0 and r < n and arr[r] == x and (forall j in [0, r) . j < n ==> arr[j] != x)
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= n
    decreases n - i
  {
    if arr[i] == x {
      r := i;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}


Problem: Write a function to check that the given string contains only a certain set of characters(in this case a-z, a-z and 0-9) by using regex.
Signature: is_allowed_specific_char(seq) -> bool
t 1
gate loops
task mbpp_210__is_allowed_specific_char(s: seq) returns (r: bool)
  ensures r == (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] >= 97 and s[i] <= 122 or s[i] >= 65 and s[i] <= 90 or s[i] >= 48 and s[i] <= 57)
{
  r := true;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i and i <= len(s)
    invariant r == (forall j in [0, i) . s[j] >= 97 and s[j] <= 122 or s[j] >= 65 and s[j] <= 90 or s[j] >= 48 and s[j] <= 57)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if not (s[i] >= 97 and s[i] <= 122 or s[i] >= 65 and s[i] <= 90 or s[i] >= 48 and s[i] <= 57) {
      r := false;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}


Problem: Write a python function to find the minimum element in a sorted and rotated array.
Signature: find_Min(seq, int, int) -> int
t 1
gate loops
task mbpp_225__find_Min(s: seq, lo: int, hi: int) returns (r: int)
  requires lo >= 0
  requires hi < len(s)
  requires lo <= hi
  ensures forall i in [lo, hi + 1) . r <= s[i]
  ensures exists i in [lo, hi + 1) . r == s[i]
{
  r := s[lo];
  var i: int := lo + 1;
  while i <= hi
    invariant i >= lo + 1
    invariant i <= hi + 1
    invariant forall j in [lo, i) . r <= s[j]
    invariant exists j in [lo, i) . r == s[j]
    decreases hi + 1 - i
  {
    if s[i] < r {
      r := s[i];
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  r := r;
}


Problem: Write a function to find minimum of three numbers.
Signature: min_of_three(int, int, int) -> int
t 0
task mbpp_227__min_of_three(a: int, b: int, c: int) returns (r: int)
  ensures r <= a
  ensures r <= b
  ensures r <= c
  ensures r == a or r == b or r == c
{
  if a <= b {
    if a <= c {
      r := a;
    } else {
      r := c;
    }
  } else {
    if b <= c {
      r := b;
    } else {
      r := c;
    }
  }
}


Problem: Write a function to replace blank spaces with any character in a string.
Signature: replace_blank(seq, int) -> seq
t 1
gate loops
task mbpp_230__replace_blank(s: seq, c: int) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) == len(s)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(s)) . if s[i] == 32 then r[i] == c else r[i] == s[i]
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant len(r) == i
    invariant forall k in [0, i) . if s[k] == 32 then r[k] == c else r[k] == s[k]
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] == 32 {
      r := r + [c];
    } else {
      r := r + [s[i]];
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

Problem: Write a python function to find the maximum of two numbers.
Signature: maximum(int, int) -> int
t 0
task mbpp_309__maximum(x: int, y: int) returns (r: int)
  ensures r == x or r == y
  ensures r >= x
  ensures r >= y
{
  if x >= y {
    r := x;
  } else {
    r := y;
  }
}

Problem: Write a function to replace all occurrences of spaces, commas, or dots with a colon in the given string by using regex.
Signature: fill_spaces(seq) -> seq
t 1
gate loops
task mbpp_542__fill_spaces(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) == len(s)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] == 32 or s[i] == 44 or s[i] == 46 ==> r[i] == 58
  ensures forall i in [0, len(s)) . not (s[i] == 32 or s[i] == 44 or s[i] == 46) ==> r[i] == s[i]
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant len(r) == i
    invariant forall k in [0, i) . s[k] == 32 or s[k] == 44 or s[k] == 46 ==> r[k] == 58
    invariant forall k in [0, i) . not (s[k] == 32 or s[k] == 44 or s[k] == 46) ==> r[k] == s[k]
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] == 32 or s[i] == 44 or s[i] == 46 {
      r := r + [58];
    } else {
      r := r + [s[i]];
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}


Problem: Write a function to toggle characters case in a string.
Signature: toggle_string(seq) -> seq
t 1
gate loops
task mbpp_557__toggle_string(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) == len(s)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(s)) . (s[i] >= 65 and s[i] <= 90 ==> r[i] == s[i] + (97 - 65)) and (s[i] >= 97 and s[i] <= 122 ==> r[i] == s[i] - (97 - 65)) and (s[i] < 65 or s[i] > 90 and s[i] < 97 or s[i] > 122 ==> r[i] == s[i])
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant len(r) == i
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= len(s)
    invariant forall j in [0, i) . (s[j] >= 65 and s[j] <= 90 ==> r[j] == s[j] + (97 - 65)) and (s[j] >= 97 and s[j] <= 122 ==> r[j] == s[j] - (97 - 65)) and (s[j] < 65 or s[j] > 90 and s[j] < 97 or s[j] > 122 ==> r[j] == s[j])
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    var c: int := s[i];
    if c >= 65 and c <= 90 {
      r := r + [c + (97 - 65)];
    } else {
      if c >= 97 and c <= 122 {
        r := r + [c - (97 - 65)];
      } else {
        r := r + [c];
      }
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}


Problem: Write a python function to find the last digit in factorial of a given number.
Signature: last_Digit_Factorial(int) -> int
t 0
task mbpp_577__last_Digit_Factorial(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures n == 0 ==> r == 1
  ensures n == 1 ==> r == 1
  ensures n == 2 ==> r == 2
  ensures n == 3 ==> r == 6
  ensures n == 4 ==> r == 4
  ensures n >= 5 ==> r == 0
{
  if n == 0 {
    r := 1;
  } else {
    if n == 1 {
      r := 1;
    } else {
      if n == 2 {
        r := 2;
      } else {
        if n == 3 {
          r := 6;
        } else {
          if n == 4 {
            r := 4;
          } else {
            r := 0;
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

Problem: Write a python function to interchange the first and last elements in a list.
Signature: swap_List(seq) -> seq
t 1
gate quantifiers
task mbpp_591__swap_List(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  requires len(s) >= 2
  ensures len(r) == len(s)
  ensures r[0] == s[len(s) - 1]
  ensures r[len(s) - 1] == s[0]
  ensures forall i in [1, len(s) - 1) . r[i] == s[i]
{
  r := s;
  r := r[0 := s[len(s) - 1]];
  r := r[len(s) - 1 := s[0]];
}

Problem: Write a function to replace whitespaces with an underscore and vice versa in a given string by using regex.
Signature: replace_spaces(seq) -> seq
t 1
gate loops
task mbpp_631__replace_spaces(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) == len(s)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(s)) . (s[i] == 32 ==> r[i] == 95) and (s[i] == 95 ==> r[i] == 32) and (s[i] != 32 and s[i] != 95 ==> r[i] == s[i])
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant len(r) == i
    invariant forall k in [0, i) . (s[k] == 32 ==> r[k] == 95) and (s[k] == 95 ==> r[k] == 32) and (s[k] != 32 and s[k] != 95 ==> r[k] == s[k])
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] == 32 {
      r := r + [95];
    } else {
      if s[i] == 95 {
        r := r + [32];
      } else {
        r := r + [s[i]];
      }
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }

Problem: Write a python function to count the number of equal numbers from three given integers.
Signature: test_three_equal(int, int, int) -> int
t 0
task mbpp_801__test_three_equal(a: int, b: int, c: int) returns (r: int)
  ensures a == b and b == c ==> r == 3
  ensures a == b and b != c ==> r == 2
  ensures a == c and c != b ==> r == 2
  ensures b == c and c != a ==> r == 2
  ensures a != b and b != c and a != c ==> r == 0
{
  if a == b and b == c {
    r := 3;
  } else {
    if a == b {
      r := 2;
    } else {
      if a == c {
        r := 2;
      } else {
        if b == c {
          r := 2;
        } else {
          r := 0;
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

t 1
gate recursion
task mbpp_873__fibonacci(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r == fibs(n)
  decreases n
spec fun fibs(n: int): int
  decreases n
= if n <= 1 then n else fibs(n - 1) + fibs(n - 2)
{
  if n == 0 {
    r := 0;
  } else {
    if n == 1 {
      r := 1;
    } else {
      r := mbpp_873__fibonacci(n - 1) + mbpp_873__fibonacci(n - 2);
    }
  }
}

Signature: flip_case(seq) -> seq
t 1
task he_27__flip_case(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) == len(s)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(s)) . (s[i] >= 65 and s[i] <= 90 ==> r[i] == s[i] - 65 + 97) and (s[i] >= 97 and s[i] <= 122 ==> r[i] == s[i] - 97 + 65) and (s[i] < 65 or s[i] > 90 and s[i] < 97 or s[i] > 122 ==> r[i] == s[i])
{
  r := [];
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant len(r) == i
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= len(s)
    invariant forall j in [0, i) . (s[j] >= 65 and s[j] <= 90 ==> r[j] == s[j] - 65 + 97) and (s[j] >= 97 and s[j] <= 122 ==> r[j] == s[j] - 97 + 65) and (s[j] < 65 or s[j] > 90 and s[j] < 97 or s[j] > 122 ==> r[j] == s[j])

      r := r + [c - 65 + 97];

        r := r + [c];
      }
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

    decreases if n_v <= n then n - n_v else n_v - n

  }

task clover_swap__swap(x: int, y: int) returns (r: (int, int))
  ensures r.0 == y
  ensures r.1 == x
{
  var x_v: int := 0;
  var y_v: int := 0;
  var tmp0: int := x;
  var tmp1: int := y;
  x_v := tmp0;
  y_v := tmp1;
  var tmp: int := x_v;
  x_v := y_v;
  y_v := tmp;
  r := (x_v, y_v);
}

task clover_swap_arith__swapArithmetic(x: int, y: int) returns (r: (int, int))
  ensures r.0 == y
  ensures r.1 == x
{
  var x_v: int := 0;
  var y_v: int := 0;
  var tmp0: int := x;
  var tmp1: int := y;
  x_v := tmp0;
  y_v := tmp1;
  x_v := y_v - x_v;
  y_v := y_v - x_v;
  x_v := y_v + x_v;
  r := (x_v, y_v);
}

      i := j;

  }

t 1
task dafny_learning_experience_tmp_tmpuxvcet_u_week1_7_maxsum__maxSum(x: int, y: int) returns (r: (int, int))
  ensures r.0 == x + y
  ensures r.1 == x or r.1 == y
  ensures x <= r.1
  ensures y <= r.1
{
  var s: int := 0;
  var m: int := 0;
  s := x + y;
  if x > y {
    m := x;
  } else {
    if y > x {
      m := y;
    } else {
      m := x;
    }
  }
  r := (s, m);
}

task dafny_verify_tmp_tmphq7j0row_ai_agent_validation_examples__computePower(n: int) returns (y: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures y >= 0
  ensures y == power(n)
spec fun power(n_v: int): int
  decreases n_v
= if n_v >= 0 then if n_v == 0 then 1 else 2 * power(n_v - 1) else 0
{
  y := 1;
  var x: int := 0;
  while x != n
    invariant 0 <= x and x <= n
    invariant y == power(x)
    invariant y >= 0
    decreases n - x
  {
    var tmp0: int := x + 1;
    var tmp1: int := y + y;
    x := tmp0;
    y := tmp1;
  }
}

task dafny_verify_tmp_tmphq7j0row_ai_agent_validation_examples__cube(n: int) returns (c: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures c >= 0
  ensures c == n * n * n
{
  c := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  var k: int := 1;
  var m: int := 6;
  while i != n
    invariant 0 <= i and i <= n
    invariant c == i * i * i
    invariant k == 3 * i * i + 3 * i + 1
    invariant m == 6 * i + 6
    invariant c >= 0
    decreases if i <= n then n - i else i - n
  {
    var tmp0: int := c + k;
    var tmp1: int := k + m;
    var tmp2: int := m + 6;
    c := tmp0;
    k := tmp1;
    m := tmp2;
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

{

  i_v2 := 1;
  while i_v2 < n
    invariant i_v2 <= n
    invariant exists j in [0, i_v2) . a[j] == min_v
    invariant forall j_v in [0, i_v2) . a[j_v] >= min_v
    decreases n - i_v2
  {
    if a[i_v2] < min_v {
      min_v := a[i_v2];
    } else {
    }
    i_v2 := i_v2 + 1;
  }
}

  var i: int := 0;

    invariant i <= n

    decreases n - i
  {

    i := i + 1;
  }
}

  ensures s == sumInts(n)
  ensures s == n * (n + 1) / 2
spec fun sumInts(n_v: int): int
  decreases n_v
= if n_v >= 0 then if n_v == 0 then 0 else sumInts(n_v - 1) + n_v else 0
{
  s := 0;
  var k: int := 0;
  while k != n
    invariant 0 <= k and k <= n
    invariant s == sumInts(k)
    invariant s == k * (k + 1) / 2
    decreases n - k
  {
    k := k + 1;
    s := s + k;
  }
}

task dafny_tmp_tmpmvs2dmry_slowmax__slow_max(a: int, b: int) returns (z: int)
  requires a >= 0
  requires b >= 0
  ensures z >= 0
  ensures z == max(a, b)
spec fun max(x: int, y: int): int
  decreases x + y
= if x >= 0 and y >= 0 then if x < y then y else x else 0
{
  z := 0;
  var x_v: int := a;
  var y_v: int := b;
  while z < x_v and z < y_v
    invariant x_v >= 0
    invariant y_v >= 0
    invariant z == a - x_v and z == b - y_v
    invariant a - x_v == b - y_v
    invariant z >= 0
    decreases x_v + y_v
  {
    z := z + 1;
    x_v := x_v - 1;
    y_v := y_v - 1;
  }
  if x_v <= y_v {
    z := b;
  } else {
    z := a;
  }
}

    y := -x;
  } else {
    y := x;
  }
}

task mieic_mfes_tmp_tmpq3ho7nve_exams_mt2_19_p4__calcR(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r >= 0
  ensures r == r_v(n)
spec fun r_v(n_v: int): int
  decreases n_v
= if n_v >= 0 then if n_v == 0 then 0 else if r_v(n_v - 1) > n_v then r_v(n_v - 1) - n_v else r_v(n_v - 1) + n_v else 0
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant 0 <= i and i <= n
    invariant r == r_v(i)
    invariant r >= 0
    decreases n - i
  {
    i := i + 1;
    if r > i {
      r := r - i;
    } else {
      r := r + i;
    }
  }
}

task metodos_formais_tmp_tmpbez22nnn_aula_2_ex1__mult(x: int, y: int) returns (r: int)
  requires x >= 0
  requires y >= 0
  ensures r >= 0
  ensures r == x * y
{
  var m: int := x;
  var n: int := y;
  r := 0;
  while m > 0
    invariant m >= 0
    invariant m * n + r == x * y
    invariant r >= 0
    decreases m - 0
  {
    r := r + n;
    m := m - 1;
  }
}

task prog_fun_solutions_tmp_tmp7_gmnz5f_extra_pow__pow(a: int, n: int) returns (y: int)
  requires a >= 0
  requires n >= 0
  ensures y >= 0
  ensures y == pow_v(a, n)
spec fun pow_v(a_v: int, e: int): int
  decreases e
= if e >= 0 then if e == 0 then 1 else a_v * pow_v(a_v, e - 1) else 0
{
  var x: int := 1;
  var k: int := 0;
  while k < n
    invariant x == pow_v(a, k)
    invariant 0 <= k and k <= n
    invariant x >= 0
    invariant k >= 0
    decreases n - k
  {
    x := a * x;
    k := k + 1;
  }
  y := x;
}

gate recursion
task prog_fun_solutions_tmp_tmp7_gmnz5f_extra_sum__sum(n: int) returns (s: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures s == sum_v(n)
spec fun sum_v(n_v: int): int
  decreases n_v
= if n_v >= 0 then if n_v == 0 then 0 else n_v + sum_v(n_v - 1) else 0
{
  var x: int := 0;
  var y: int := 1;
  var k: int := n;
  while k > 0
    invariant sum_v(n) == x + y * sum_v(k)
    invariant 0 <= k and k <= n
    invariant x >= 0
    invariant k >= 0
    decreases k
  {
    x := x + y * k;
    k := k - 1;
  }
  s := x;
}

task prog_fun_solutions_tmp_tmp7_gmnz5f_mockexam2_p2__problem2(p: int, q: int, x: int, y: int) returns (r: (int, int))
  requires p == 2 * x + y
  requires q == x + 3
  ensures r.0 == x
  ensures r.1 == y
{
  var r_v: int := 0;
  var s: int := 0;
  var tmp0: int := p;
  var tmp1: int := q;
  r_v := tmp0;
  s := tmp1;
  r_v := r_v - 2 * s + 6;
  s := s - 3;
  var tmp0_v: int := s;
  var tmp1_v: int := r_v;
  r_v := tmp0_v;
  s := tmp1_v;
  r := (r_v, s);
}

  requires x >= 0
  requires 2 * m == 1 - x or m == x + 3
  ensures r == x
{
  r := m;
  if 1 - 2 * r >= 0 {
    r := 2 * r;
    r := -r + 1;
  } else {
    r := r - 3;
  }
}

task programmverifikation_und_synthese_tmp_tmppurk6ime_pvs_assignment_ex_07_hoangkim_ex07_hoangkim__findMin(a: seq, lo: int) returns (minIdx: int)
  requires lo >= 0
  requires len(a) > 0
  requires lo < len(a)
  ensures minIdx >= 0
  ensures lo <= minIdx
  ensures minIdx < len(a)
  ensures forall x in [lo, len(a)) . a[minIdx] <= a[x]
{
  var j: int := lo;
  minIdx := lo;
  while j < len(a)
    invariant lo <= j and j <= len(a)
    invariant lo <= minIdx and minIdx < len(a)
    invariant forall k in [lo, j) . a[k] >= a[minIdx]
    invariant minIdx >= 0
    decreases len(a) - j
  {
    if a[j] < a[minIdx] {
      minIdx := j;
    } else {
    }
    j := j + 1;
  }
}

task programmverifikation_und_synthese_tmp_tmppurk6ime_pvs_assignment_ex_07_hoangkim_ex07_hoangkim__swap(a: seq, i: int, j: int) returns (a_out: seq)
  requires i >= 0
  requires j >= 0
  requires len(a) > 0
  requires i < len(a)
  requires j < len(a)
  ensures len(a_out) == len(a)
  ensures a_out[i] == a[j]
  ensures a_out[j] == a[i]
{
  a_out := a;
  var tmp0: int := a_out[j];
  var tmp1: int := a_out[i];
  a_out := a_out[i := tmp0];
  a_out := a_out[j := tmp1];
}

  ensures forall x_v in [0, len(s_out)) . s[x_v] >= 0 ==> s_out[x_v] == s[x_v]
{
  s_out := s;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s_out)
    invariant len(s_out) == len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i and i <= len(s_out)
    invariant forall k in [0, i) . s[k] < 0 ==> s_out[k] == -s[k]
    invariant forall k_v in [0, i) . s[k_v] >= 0 ==> s_out[k_v] == s[k_v]
    invariant forall k_v2 in [i, len(s_out)) . s[k_v2] == s_out[k_v2]
    decreases len(s_out) - i
  {
    if s_out[i] < 0 {
      s_out := s_out[i := -s_out[i]];
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task tfg_tmp_tmpbvsao41w_algoritmos_dafny_div_ent_it__div_ent_it(a: int, b: int) returns (r: (int, int))
  requires a >= 0
  requires b > 0
  ensures a == b * r.0 + r.1
  ensures 0 <= r.1 and r.1 < b
{
  var c: int := 0;
  var r_v: int := 0;
  c := 0;
  r_v := a;
  while r_v >= b
    invariant a == b * c + r_v and r_v >= 0 and b > 0
    decreases r_v
  {
    c := c + 1;
    r_v := r_v - b;
  }
  r := (c, r_v);
}

task dafny_language_server_tmp_tmpkir0kenl_test_dafny1_cubes__cubes(a: seq) returns (a_out: seq)
  ensures len(a_out) == len(a)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(a_out)) . a_out[i] == i * i * i
{
  a_out := a;
  var n: int := 0;
  var c: int := 0;
  var k: int := 1;
  var m: int := 6;
  while n < len(a_out)
    invariant len(a_out) == len(a)
    invariant 0 <= n and n <= len(a_out)
    invariant forall i_v in [0, n) . a_out[i_v] == i_v * i_v * i_v
    invariant c == n * n * n
    invariant k == 3 * n * n + 3 * n + 1
    invariant m == 6 * n + 6
    decreases len(a_out) - n
  {
    a_out := a_out[n := c];
    c := c + k;
    k := k + m;
    m := m + 6;
    n := n + 1;
  }
}

task dafny_programs_tmp_tmpcwodh6qh_src_factorial__factorial(n: int) returns (res: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures res >= 0
  ensures res == fact(n)
spec fun fact(n_v: int): int
  decreases n_v
= if n_v >= 0 then if n_v == 0 then 1 else n_v * fact(n_v - 1) else 0
{
  var i: int := 1;
  res := 1;
  while i < n + 1
    invariant 0 < i and i <= n + 1
    invariant res == fact(i - 1)
    invariant res >= 0
    decreases n + 1 - i
  {
    res := i * res;
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task dafny_synthesis_task_id_145__maxDifference(a: seq) returns (diff: int)
  requires len(a) > 1
  ensures forall i in [0, len(a)) . forall j in [0, len(a)) . a[i] - a[j] <= diff
{
  var minVal: int := a[0];
  var maxVal: int := a[0];
  var h: int := len(a);
  var i_v: int := 1;
  while i_v < h
    invariant 1 <= i_v
    invariant i_v <= h
    invariant 1 <= i_v and i_v <= len(a)
    invariant minVal <= maxVal
    invariant forall k in [0, i_v) . minVal <= a[k] and a[k] <= maxVal
    decreases h - i_v
  {
    if a[i_v] < minVal {
      minVal := a[i_v];
    } else {
      if a[i_v] > maxVal {
        maxVal := a[i_v];
      } else {
      }
    }
    i_v := i_v + 1;
  }
  diff := maxVal - minVal;
}

task dafny_synthesis_task_id_261__elementWiseDivision(a: seq, b: seq) returns (result: seq)

task t1_mf_tmp_tmpi_sqie4j_exemplos_introducao_ex4__fatorial(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures r >= 0
  ensures r == fat(n)
spec fun fat(n_v: int): int
  decreases n_v
= if n_v >= 0 then if n_v == 0 then 1 else n_v * fat(n_v - 1) else 0
{
  r := 1;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant 0 <= i and i <= n
    invariant r == fat(i)
    invariant r >= 0
    decreases n - i
  {
    i := i + 1;
    r := r * i;
  }

Problem: Return list with elements incremented by 1.
Signature: humaneval_dafny_042_incr_list__incr_list(seq) -> seq
t 1
gate loops
task humaneval_dafny_042_incr_list__incr_list(l: seq) returns (result: seq)
  ensures len(result) == len(l)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(l)) . result[i] == l[i] + 1
{
  result := [];
  var i_v: int := 0;
  while i_v < len(l)
    invariant 0 <= i_v and i_v <= len(l)
    invariant len(result) == i_v
    invariant forall i1 in [0, i_v) . result[i1] == l[i1] + 1
    decreases len(l) - i_v
  {
    result := result + [l[i_v] + 1];
    i_v := i_v + 1;
  }
}

Problem: Given n browser tabs indexed 1 to n with cursor at position pos, find minimum time to close all tabs except those in range [l, r]. Operations: move cursor (1 sec), close all tabs to left of cursor (1 sec), close all tabs to right of cursor (1 sec).
Signature: vericoding_da0001__solve(int, int, int, int) -> int
t 1
gate recursion
task vericoding_da0001__solve(n: int, pos: int, l: int, r: int) returns (result: int)
  requires validInput(n, pos, l, r)
  ensures result >= 0
  ensures noTabsToClose(l, r, n) ==> result == 0
  ensures onlyCloseRight(l, r, n) ==> result == abs(pos - r) + 1
  ensures onlyCloseLeft(l, r, n) ==> result == abs(pos - l) + 1
  ensures closeBothSides(l, r, n) and (l <= pos and pos <= r) and pos - l < r - pos ==> result == pos - l + 1 + (r - l) + 1
  ensures closeBothSides(l, r, n) and (l <= pos and pos <= r) and pos - l >= r - pos ==> result == r - pos + 1 + (r - l) + 1
  ensures closeBothSides(l, r, n) and pos > r ==> result == pos - r + 1 + (r - l) + 1
  ensures closeBothSides(l, r, n) and pos < l ==> result == l - pos + 1 + (r - l) + 1
  ensures result <= 2 * n
spec fun abs(x: int): int
  decreases 0
= if x >= 0 then x else -x
spec fun noTabsToClose(l_v: int, r_v: int, n_v: int): bool
  decreases 0
= l_v == 1 and r_v == n_v
spec fun onlyCloseRight(l_v2: int, r_v2: int, n_v2: int): bool
  decreases 0
= l_v2 == 1 and r_v2 < n_v2
spec fun validInput(n_v3: int, pos_v: int, l_v3: int, r_v3: int): bool
  decreases 0
= (1 <= n_v3 and n_v3 <= 100) and (1 <= pos_v and pos_v <= n_v3) and (1 <= l_v3 and l_v3 <= r_v3 and r_v3 <= n_v3)
spec fun onlyCloseLeft(l_v4: int, r_v4: int, n_v4: int): bool
  decreases 0
= l_v4 > 1 and r_v4 == n_v4
spec fun closeBothSides(l_v5: int, r_v5: int, n_v5: int): bool
  decreases 0
= l_v5 > 1 and r_v5 < n_v5
{
  if l == 1 and r == n {
    result := 0;
  } else {
    if l == 1 and r < n {
      result := abs(pos - r) + 1;
    } else {
      if l > 1 and r == n {
        result := abs(pos - l) + 1;
      } else {
        if pos < l {
          result := l - pos + 1 + r - l + 1;
        } else {
          if pos > r {
            result := pos - r + 1 + r - l + 1;
          } else {
            if pos - l < r - pos {
              result := pos - l + 1 + r - l + 1;
            } else {
              result := r - pos + 1 + r - l + 1;
            }
          }
        }
      }
    }
  }
}

Problem: Given three tasks with integer values A1, A2, A3, find the minimum cost to complete all tasks. Rules: Complete any one task first at cost 0, then completing task i followed by task j costs |Aj - Ai|. All tasks must be completed.
Signature: vericoding_da0533__solve(int, int, int) -> int
t 1
gate recursion
task vericoding_da0533__solve(a1: int, a2: int, a3: int) returns (result: int)
  requires validInput(a1, a2, a3)
  ensures result >= 0
  ensures result == minimumCost(a1, a2, a3)
spec fun validInput(a1_v: int, a2_v: int, a3_v: int): bool
  decreases 0
= (1 <= a1_v and a1_v <= 100) and (1 <= a2_v and a2_v <= 100) and (1 <= a3_v and a3_v <= 100)
spec fun minOfThree(a1_v2: int, a2_v2: int, a3_v2: int): int
  decreases 0
= if a1_v2 <= a2_v2 and a1_v2 <= a3_v2 then a1_v2 else if a2_v2 <= a3_v2 then a2_v2 else a3_v2
spec fun maxOfThree(a1_v3: int, a2_v3: int, a3_v3: int): int
  decreases 0
= if a1_v3 >= a2_v3 and a1_v3 >= a3_v3 then a1_v3 else if a2_v3 >= a3_v3 then a2_v3 else a3_v3
spec fun minimumCost(a1_v4: int, a2_v4: int, a3_v4: int): int
  decreases 0
= maxOfThree(a1_v4, a2_v4, a3_v4) - minOfThree(a1_v4, a2_v4, a3_v4)
{
  var max: int := a1;
  var min: int := a1;
  if a2 > max {
    max := a2;
  } else {
  }
  if a3 > max {
    max := a3;
  } else {
  }
  if a2 < min {
    min := a2;
  } else {
  }
  if a3 < min {
    min := a3;
  } else {
  }
  result := max - min;
}

  decreases 0

Signature: vericoding_da0668__solve(int, int, int) -> int
t 1
gate recursion
task vericoding_da0668__solve(a: int, b: int, c: int) returns (result: int)
  requires validInput(a, b, c)
  ensures result == correctResult(a, b, c)
spec fun validInput(a_v: int, b_v: int, c_v: int): bool
  decreases 0
= (1 <= a_v and a_v <= 10000) and (1 <= b_v and b_v <= 10000) and (1 <= c_v and c_v <= 10000)
spec fun minOfThree(x: int, y: int, z: int): int
  decreases 0
= if x <= y and x <= z then x else if y <= z then y else z
spec fun correctResult(a_v2: int, b_v2: int, c_v2: int): int
  decreases 0
= minOfThree(a_v2 + b_v2, a_v2 + c_v2, b_v2 + c_v2)
{
  var sum1: int := a + b;
  var sum2: int := a + c;
  var sum3: int := b + c;
  if sum1 <= sum2 and sum1 <= sum3 {
    result := sum1;
  } else {
    if sum2 <= sum3 {
      result := sum2;
    } else {
      result := sum3;
    }
  }

Problem: Given three integers representing colors, count the number of distinct colors. Input constraints: 1 ≤ a,b,c ≤ 100 Output: number of different kinds of colors (1, 2, or 3)

gate recursion

  ensures 1 <= result
  ensures result <= 3
  ensures result == countDistinctColors(a, b, c)
  ensures (result == 1) == allSame(a, b, c)
  ensures (result == 2) == exactlyTwoSame(a, b, c)
  ensures (result == 3) == allDifferent(a, b, c)
spec fun countDistinctColors(a_v: int, b_v: int, c_v: int): int
  decreases 0
= if a_v == b_v and b_v == c_v then 1 else if a_v == b_v or b_v == c_v or a_v == c_v then 2 else 3
spec fun allSame(a_v2: int, b_v2: int, c_v2: int): bool
  decreases 0
= a_v2 == b_v2 and b_v2 == c_v2
spec fun allDifferent(a_v3: int, b_v3: int, c_v3: int): bool
  decreases 0
= a_v3 != b_v3 and b_v3 != c_v3 and a_v3 != c_v3
spec fun validInput(a_v4: int, b_v4: int, c_v4: int): bool
  decreases 0

spec fun exactlyTwoSame(a_v5: int, b_v5: int, c_v5: int): bool
  decreases 0
= a_v5 == b_v5 and b_v5 != c_v5 or b_v5 == c_v5 and a_v5 != b_v5 or a_v5 == c_v5 and a_v5 != b_v5
{
  if a == b and b == c {
    result := 1;
  } else {
    if a == b or b == c or a == c {
      result := 2;
    } else {
      result := 3;
    }
  }
}

  requires n >= 0
  ensures s == n * (n + 1) / 2
{

  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant 0 <= i and i <= n
    invariant s == i * (i + 1) / 2
    decreases n - i
  {

  }

    } else {

    i := i + 1;

task vericoding_dd0539__reverse(a: seq) returns (aRev: seq)
  ensures len(aRev) == len(a)
  ensures len(aRev) == len(a)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(a)) . a[i] == aRev[len(aRev) - i - 1]
{
  aRev := seq(len(a), 0);
  var i_v: int := 0;
  while i_v < len(a)
    invariant len(aRev) == len(a)
    invariant 0 <= i_v and i_v <= len(a)
    invariant forall j in [0, i_v) . aRev[len(a) - j - 1] == a[j]
    decreases len(a) - i_v
  {
    aRev := aRev[len(a) - i_v - 1 := a[i_v]];
    i_v := i_v + 1;
  }
}

task vericoding_dd0847__divMod1(a: int, b: int) returns (r: (int, int))
  requires b > 0
  requires a >= 0

  ensures 0 <= r.1 and r.1 < b

    invariant r_v >= 0

  }

task vericoding_dj0014__myfun(a: seq, n: int) returns (a_out: seq)

    decreases n - i
  {
    if n % 2 == 0 {
      if a_out[i] % 2 == 1 {
        a_out := a_out[i := a_out[i] + 1];
      } else {
      }
    } else {
      if a_out[i] % 2 == 0 {
        a_out := a_out[i := a_out[i] + 1];
      } else {
      }
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

task vericoding_ds0010__clip(a: seq, min: int, max: int) returns (result: seq)
  requires min < max
  ensures len(result) == len(a)
  ensures len(result) == len(a)
  ensures forall i in [0, len(a)) . if a[i] < min then result[i] == min else if a[i] > max then result[i] == max else result[i] == a[i]
{
  result := seq(len(a), 0);
  var i_v: int := 0;
  while i_v < len(a)
    invariant len(result) == len(a)
    invariant 0 <= i_v and i_v <= len(a)
    invariant forall j in [0, i_v) . if a[j] < min then result[j] == min else if a[j] > max then result[j] == max else result[j] == a[j]
    decreases len(a) - i_v
  {
    if a[i_v] < min {
      result := result[i_v := min];
    } else {
      if a[i_v] > max {
        result := result[i_v := max];
      } else {
        result := result[i_v := a[i_v]];
      }
    }
    i_v := i_v + 1;
  }
}

Problem: /* This task requires writing a Dafny method that returns the nth "ugly number". Ugly numbers are positive integers whose only prime factors are 2, 3, or 5. The method should generate ugly numbers in ascending order and return the nth one. The first ugly number is 1. Input: The input is a natural number: n: The index (1-based) of the ugly number to return. Output: The output is a natural number: The nth smallest ugly number. */
Signature: vericoding_dv0057__nthUglyNumber(int) -> int
t 1
gate loops
task vericoding_dv0057__nthUglyNumber(n: int) returns (result: int)
  requires n > 0
  ensures result > 0
{
  var count: int := 0;
  var current: int := 1;
  result := 1;
  var bound: int := n * 100;
  while count < n and current < bound
    invariant 0 <= count and count <= n
    invariant current >= 1
    invariant result >= 1
    decreases bound - current
  {
    var candidate: int := current;
    while candidate % 2 == 0 and candidate > 1
      invariant candidate >= 1
      decreases candidate
    {
      candidate := candidate / 2;
    }
    while candidate % 3 == 0 and candidate > 1
      invariant candidate >= 1
      decreases candidate
    {
      candidate := candidate / 3;
    }
    while candidate % 5 == 0 and candidate > 1
      invariant candidate >= 1
      decreases candidate
    {
      candidate := candidate / 5;
    }
    if candidate == 1 {
      count := count + 1;
      if count == n {
        result := current;
      } else {
      }
    } else {
    }
    current := current + 1;
  }
}

Problem: /* This task requires writing a Dafny method that finds the minimum among three given integers. The method should return the smallest value, ensuring that the result is less than or equal to each of the input numbers and that it is one of the provided integers. Input: The input consists of three integers: a: The first integer. b: The second integer. c: The third integer. Output: The output is an integer: Returns the minimum of the three input numbers, assuring that the returned value is less than or equal to a, b, and c, and that it matches one of these values. */
Signature: vericoding_dv0086__minOfThree(int, int, int) -> int
t 1
task vericoding_dv0086__minOfThree(a: int, b: int, c: int) returns (result: int)
  ensures result <= a
  ensures result <= b
  ensures result <= c
  ensures result == a or result == b or result == c
{
  if a <= b and a <= c {
    result := a;
  } else {
    if b <= a and b <= c {
      result := b;
    } else {
      result := c;
    }
  }
}

  ensures forall i in [0, len(s)) . result[i] == 2 * s[i]

  }

Problem: Given a non-negative integer n, round it to the nearest integer that ends with 0. If n already ends with 0, return n unchanged. When there are two equally distant options (when the last digit is 5), use banker's rounding (round half to even).
Signature: vericoding_da0069__solve(int) -> int
t 1
gate recursion
task vericoding_da0069__solve(n: int) returns (result: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures validResult(n, result)
spec fun validResult(n_v: int, result_v: int): bool
  decreases 0
= if n_v >= 0 then result_v % 10 == 0 and result_v >= 0 and (n_v % 10 < 5 ==> result_v == n_v / 10 * 10) and (n_v % 10 > 5 ==> result_v == (n_v / 10 + 1) * 10) and (n_v % 10 == 5 ==> (n_v / 10 % 2 == 0 ==> result_v == n_v / 10 * 10) and (n_v / 10 % 2 == 1 ==> result_v == (n_v / 10 + 1) * 10)) else false
{
  var quotient: int := n / 10;
  var remainder: int := n % 10;
  if remainder < 5 {
    result := quotient * 10;
  } else {
    if remainder > 5 {
      result := (quotient + 1) * 10;
    } else {
      if quotient % 2 == 0 {
        result := quotient * 10;
      } else {
        result := (quotient + 1) * 10;
      }
    }
  }
}

t 1
gate recursion
task vericoding_da0070__solve(n: int, k: int) returns (result: seq)
  requires validInput(n, k)
  ensures validOutput(result, n, k)
  ensures result[0] == minGoodApartments(n, k)
  ensures result[1] == maxGoodApartments(n, k)
spec fun validInput(n_v: int, k_v: int): bool
  decreases 0
= n_v >= 1 and k_v >= 0 and k_v <= n_v
spec fun validOutput(result_v: seq, n_v2: int, k_v2: int): bool
  decreases 0
= len(result_v) == 2 and result_v[0] >= 0 and result_v[1] >= 0 and result_v[0] <= result_v[1] and result_v[0] <= n_v2 - k_v2 and result_v[1] <= n_v2 - k_v2
spec fun maxGoodApartments(n_v3: int, k_v3: int): int
  decreases 0
= if validInput(n_v3, k_v3) then if k_v3 == 0 or k_v3 == n_v3 then 0 else if n_v3 - k_v3 < k_v3 * 2 then n_v3 - k_v3 else k_v3 * 2 else 0

{
  var min: int := if k == 0 or k == n then 0 else 1;
  var max: int := if k == 0 or k == n then 0 else if n - k < k * 2 then n - k else k * 2;
  result := [min, max];
}


Problem: Given k_2 digits '2', k_3 digits '3', k_5 digits '5', and k_6 digits '6', form integers 32 and 256 to maximize their sum. Each digit can only be used once. To form 256: need one '2', one '5', one '6' To form 32: need one '3', one '2'
Signature: vericoding_da0075__solve(int, int, int, int) -> int
t 1
gate recursion
task vericoding_da0075__solve(k2: int, k3: int, k5: int, k6: int) returns (result: int)
  requires validInput(k2, k3, k5, k6)
  ensures result >= 0
  ensures result == optimalSum(k2, k3, k5, k6)
spec fun min(a: int, b: int): int
  decreases 0
= if a <= b then a else b
spec fun validInput(k2_v: int, k3_v: int, k5_v: int, k6_v: int): bool
  decreases 0
= k2_v >= 0 and k3_v >= 0 and k5_v >= 0 and k6_v >= 0 and k2_v <= 5000000 and k3_v <= 5000000 and k5_v <= 5000000 and k6_v <= 5000000
spec fun optimalSum(k2_v2: int, k3_v2: int, k5_v2: int, k6_v2: int): int
  decreases 0
= if validInput(k2_v2, k3_v2, k5_v2, k6_v2) then 256 * min(min(k2_v2, k5_v2), k6_v2) + 32 * min(k3_v2, k2_v2 - min(min(k2_v2, k5_v2), k6_v2)) else 0
spec fun min3(a_v: int, b_v: int, c: int): int
  decreases 0
= min(min(a_v, b_v), c)
{
  var count256: int := min3(k2, k5, k6);
  var remaining_k2: int := k2 - count256;
  var count32: int := min(k3, remaining_k2);
  result := 256 * count256 + 32 * count32;
}

Signature: vericoding_da0132__solve(int, int, int, int, int) -> int
t 1
gate recursion
task vericoding_da0132__solve(a: int, b: int, x: int, y: int, z: int) returns (result: int)

  ensures result >= 0
  ensures result == minAdditionalCrystals(a, b, x, y, z)
spec fun validInput(a_v: int, b_v: int, x_v: int, y_v: int, z_v: int): bool
  decreases 0
= a_v >= 0 and b_v >= 0 and x_v >= 0 and y_v >= 0 and z_v >= 0
spec fun yellowCrystalsNeeded(x_v2: int, y_v2: int): int
  decreases 0
= x_v2 * 2 + y_v2
spec fun blueCrystalsNeeded(y_v3: int, z_v2: int): int
  decreases 0
= y_v3 + z_v2 * 3
spec fun max(a_v2: int, b_v2: int): int
  decreases 0
= if a_v2 >= b_v2 then a_v2 else b_v2
spec fun minAdditionalCrystals(a_v3: int, b_v3: int, x_v3: int, y_v4: int, z_v3: int): int
  decreases 0

  var yellowNeeded: int := x * 2 + y;
  var blueNeeded: int := y + z * 3;
  var additionalYellow: int := if yellowNeeded > a then yellowNeeded - a else 0;
  var additionalBlue: int := if blueNeeded > b then blueNeeded - b else 0;
  result := additionalYellow + additionalBlue;
}

spec fun validInput(n_v: int, v_v: int): bool
  decreases 0
= (2 <= n_v and n_v <= 100) and (1 <= v_v and v_v <= 100)
spec fun minCost(n_v2: int, v_v2: int): int
  decreases 0
= if validInput(n_v2, v_v2) then if n_v2 - 1 <= v_v2 then n_v2 - 1 else v_v2 + (n_v2 - 1 - v_v2) * (n_v2 - 1 - v_v2 + 3) / 2 else 0

  var req: int := n - 1;
  if req <= v {
    result := req;
  } else {
    var remaining: int := req - v;
    result := v + remaining * (remaining + 3) / 2;
  }
}

Problem: Given four integers representing sleep requirements and alarm timing, simulate an alarm system where: first alarm rings after b minutes, if total sleep >= a then wake permanently, otherwise set alarm for c minutes later and spend d minutes falling asleep. If alarm rings while falling asleep (c <= d), reset and repeat. Find total time until permanent wake or -1 if never happens.
Signature: vericoding_da0419__solve(int, int, int, int) -> int
t 1
gate recursion
task vericoding_da0419__solve(a: int, b: int, c: int, d: int) returns (result: int)
  requires validInput(a, b, c, d)
  ensures firstAlarmSufficient(a, b) ==> result == b
  ensures neverWakes(a, b, c, d) ==> result == -(1)
  ensures eventuallyWakes(a, b, c, d) ==> result == calculateWakeTime(a, b, c, d)
spec fun firstAlarmSufficient(a_v: int, b_v: int): bool
  decreases 0
= a_v <= b_v
spec fun validInput(a_v2: int, b_v2: int, c_v: int, d_v: int): bool
  decreases 0
= a_v2 >= 0 and b_v2 >= 0 and c_v >= 0 and d_v >= 0

  decreases 0

spec fun eventuallyWakes(a_v4: int, b_v4: int, c_v3: int, d_v3: int): bool
  decreases 0
= a_v4 > b_v4 and c_v3 > d_v3
spec fun calculateWakeTime(a_v5: int, b_v5: int, c_v4: int, d_v4: int): int
  decreases 0
= if validInput(a_v5, b_v5, c_v4, d_v4) and eventuallyWakes(a_v5, b_v5, c_v4, d_v4) then b_v5 + c_v4 * ((a_v5 - b_v5 - 1) / (c_v4 - d_v4) + 1) else 0
{
  if a <= b {
    result := b;
  } else {
    if c <= d {
      result := -(1);
    } else {
      var remaining: int := a - b;
      var cycles: int := (remaining - 1) / (c - d) + 1;
      result := b + c * cycles;
    }
  }
}

Problem: Given vanilla cookies (a), chocolate cookies (b), type-1 guests (n), and type-2 guests (m), determine if there exists an ordering of all guests such that no guest gets angry. Type-1 guests choose vanilla if v > c, else chocolate. Type-2 guests choose chocolate if v > c, else vanilla. A guest gets angry if their chosen cookie type has 0 cookies available. // First check: must have enough total cookies // Second check: type-2 guests are the limiting factor // They will take from the minority cookie type, so we need // enough of the minority type to satisfy all type-2 guests
Signature: vericoding_da0455__solveCookieDistribution(int, int, int, int) -> bool
t 1
gate recursion
task vericoding_da0455__solveCookieDistribution(a: int, b: int, n: int, m: int) returns (result: bool)
  requires a >= 0
  requires b >= 0
  requires n >= 0
  requires m >= 0
  requires validTestCase(a, b, n, m)
  ensures result == canSatisfyAllGuests(a, b, n, m)
  ensures result ==> a + b >= n + m and m <= min(a, b)
  ensures not result ==> a + b < n + m or m > min(a, b)
spec fun min(x: int, y: int): int
  decreases 0
= if x >= 0 and y >= 0 then if x <= y then x else y else 0
spec fun canSatisfyAllGuests(a_v: int, b_v: int, n_v: int, m_v: int): bool
  decreases 0
= if a_v >= 0 and b_v >= 0 and n_v >= 0 and m_v >= 0 then a_v + b_v >= n_v + m_v and m_v <= min(a_v, b_v) else false
spec fun validTestCase(a_v2: int, b_v2: int, n_v2: int, m_v2: int): bool
  decreases 0
= if a_v2 >= 0 and b_v2 >= 0 and n_v2 >= 0 and m_v2 >= 0 then n_v2 + m_v2 > 0 else false
{
  if a + b < n + m {
    result := false;
    return result;
  } else {
  }
  var minCookies: int := if a <= b then a else b;
  if m <= minCookies {
    result := true;
  } else {
    result := false;
  }
}


Problem: Given two rectangles with width W, where Rectangle 1 spans horizontally [a, a+W] and Rectangle 2 spans horizontally [b, b+W], find the minimum horizontal distance Rectangle 2 must be moved so that the two rectangles connect (overlap or touch).
Signature: vericoding_da0621__solve(int, int, int) -> int
t 1
gate recursion
task vericoding_da0621__solve(w: int, a: int, b: int) returns (result: int)
  requires validInput(w, a, b)
  ensures result == minMoveDistance(w, a, b)
  ensures result >= 0
  ensures rectanglesConnect(w, a, b) == (result == 0)
spec fun absDiff(x: int, y: int): int
  decreases 0
= if x >= y then x - y else y - x
spec fun validInput(w_v: int, a_v: int, b_v: int): bool
  decreases 0
= w_v >= 1 and a_v >= 1 and b_v >= 1
spec fun rectanglesConnect(w_v2: int, a_v2: int, b_v2: int): bool
  decreases 0
= if validInput(w_v2, a_v2, b_v2) then absDiff(a_v2, b_v2) <= w_v2 else false
spec fun minMoveDistance(w_v3: int, a_v3: int, b_v3: int): int
  decreases 0
= if validInput(w_v3, a_v3, b_v3) then if absDiff(a_v3, b_v3) <= w_v3 then 0 else absDiff(a_v3, b_v3) - w_v3 else 0
{
  var distance: int := absDiff(a, b);
  if distance <= w {
    result := 0;
  } else {
    result := distance - w;
  }
}

spec fun minTotalFare(a_v2: int, b_v2: int, c_v2: int, d_v2: int): int
  decreases 0
= (if a_v2 < b_v2 then a_v2 else b_v2) + (if c_v2 < d_v2 then c_v2 else d_v2)
{
  var minAB: int := if a < b then a else b;
  var minCD: int := if c < d then c else d;
  result := minAB + minCD;
}


Problem: Given N S-shaped pieces and M c-shaped pieces, find the maximum number of "Scc groups" that can be formed. Each Scc group requires exactly 1 S-shaped piece and 2 c-shaped pieces. Additionally, 2 c-shaped pieces can be combined to create 1 S-shaped piece.
Signature: vericoding_da0661__solve(int, int) -> int
t 1
gate recursion
task vericoding_da0661__solve(n: int, m: int) returns (result: int)
  requires validInput(n, m)
  ensures result >= 0
  ensures result == maxSccGroups(n, m)
spec fun validInput(n_v: int, m_v: int): bool
  decreases 0
= n_v >= 0 and m_v >= 0
spec fun maxSccGroups(n_v2: int, m_v2: int): int
  decreases 0
= if validInput(n_v2, m_v2) then (if n_v2 < m_v2 / 2 then n_v2 else m_v2 / 2) + (m_v2 - (if n_v2 < m_v2 / 2 then n_v2 else m_v2 / 2) * 2) / 4 else 0
{
  var directGroups: int := if n < m / 2 then n else m / 2;
  var remainingCPieces: int := m - directGroups * 2;
  var additionalGroups: int := remainingCPieces / 4;
  result := directGroups + additionalGroups;
}

t 1

task vericoding_da0083__solve(n: int) returns (result: int)

  ensures result == minBills(n)

  decreases 0

  decreases 0

  result := hundreds + twenties + tens + fives + ones;

Problem: Given uncovered heights of two adjacent towers after snowfall, find the snow depth. Towers have triangular number heights: 1, 3, 6, 10, ... (i-th tower height = i*(i+1)/2). Snow covers uniformly to same depth everywhere.
Signature: vericoding_da0528__solve(int, int) -> int
t 1
gate recursion
task vericoding_da0528__solve(a: int, b: int) returns (result: int)
  requires validInput(a, b)
  requires validSnowDepth(a, b, ((b - a) * (b - a) - (a + b)) / 2)
  ensures result >= 1
  ensures result == snowDepthFormula(a, b)
spec fun validInput(a_v: int, b_v: int): bool
  decreases 0
= a_v >= 1 and b_v > a_v and b_v < 499500
spec fun validSnowDepth(a_v2: int, b_v2: int, depth: int): bool
  decreases 0
= depth >= 1 and (b_v2 - a_v2) * (b_v2 - a_v2) - (a_v2 + b_v2) >= 2 and ((b_v2 - a_v2) * (b_v2 - a_v2) - (a_v2 + b_v2)) % 2 == 0
spec fun snowDepthFormula(a_v3: int, b_v3: int): int
  decreases 0
= if validInput(a_v3, b_v3) and validSnowDepth(a_v3, b_v3, ((b_v3 - a_v3) * (b_v3 - a_v3) - (a_v3 + b_v3)) / 2) then ((b_v3 - a_v3) * (b_v3 - a_v3) - (a_v3 + b_v3)) / 2 else 0

  var diff: int := b - a;
  var sum: int := a + b;
  result := (diff * diff - sum) / 2;
}

Problem: Given three distinct integers representing positions of three friends on a number line, find the minimum total distance they need to travel to meet at a single point.

t 1
gate recursion
task vericoding_va0085__find_minimum_total_distance(x1: int, x2: int, x3: int) returns (result: int)
  requires valid_input(x1, x2, x3)
  ensures result == min_total_distance(x1, x2, x3)
  ensures result >= 1
  ensures result <= 99
spec fun min_total_distance(x1_v: int, x2_v: int, x3_v: int): int
  decreases 0
= (if x1_v >= x2_v and x1_v >= x3_v then x1_v else if x2_v >= x1_v and x2_v >= x3_v then x2_v else x3_v) - (if x1_v <= x2_v and x1_v <= x3_v then x1_v else if x2_v <= x1_v and x2_v <= x3_v then x2_v else x3_v)
spec fun valid_input(x1_v2: int, x2_v2: int, x3_v2: int): bool
  decreases 0
= (1 <= x1_v2 and x1_v2 <= 100) and (1 <= x2_v2 and x2_v2 <= 100) and (1 <= x3_v2 and x3_v2 <= 100) and x1_v2 != x2_v2 and x1_v2 != x3_v2 and x2_v2 != x3_v2
{
  var max_pos_i8: int := if x1 >= x2 and x1 >= x3 then x1 else if x2 >= x1 and x2 >= x3 then x2 else x3;
  var min_pos_i8: int := if x1 <= x2 and x1 <= x3 then x1 else if x2 <= x1 and x2 <= x3 then x2 else x3;
  var diff_i8: int := max_pos_i8 - min_pos_i8;
  result := diff_i8;
}

    if b == 2 {

      }

        if b == 2 {

        }

Problem: Given two time intervals [A, B) and [C, D), find the length of their intersection. Alice holds a button from time A to time B (exclusive). Bob holds a button from time C to time D (exclusive). Calculate how many seconds both are holding their buttons simultaneously.
Signature: vericoding_va0530__solve(int, int, int, int) -> int
t 1
gate recursion
task vericoding_va0530__solve(a: int, b: int, c: int, d: int) returns (result: int)
  requires valid_input(a, b, c, d)
  ensures result >= 0
  ensures result == interval_overlap_length(a, b, c, d)
  ensures result <= 100
spec fun interval_overlap_length(a_v: int, b_v: int, c_v: int, d_v: int): int
  decreases 0
= if (if b_v <= d_v then b_v else d_v) - (if a_v >= c_v then a_v else c_v) > 0 then (if b_v <= d_v then b_v else d_v) - (if a_v >= c_v then a_v else c_v) else 0
spec fun valid_input(a_v2: int, b_v2: int, c_v2: int, d_v2: int): bool
  decreases 0
= (0 <= a_v2 and a_v2 < b_v2 and b_v2 <= 100) and (0 <= c_v2 and c_v2 < d_v2 and d_v2 <= 100)
{
  var m1_i8: int := if b < d then b else d;
  var m1_i8_v: int := if a > c then a else c;
  var overlap_i8: int := m1_i8 - m1_i8_v;
  var res_i8: int := if overlap_i8 > 0 then overlap_i8 else 0;
  result := res_i8;
}

  assert 1 <= (if x1_v3 <= x2_v3 and x1_v3 <= x3_v3 then x1_v3 else if x2_v3 <= x1_v3 and x2_v3 <= x3_v3 then x2_v3 else x3_v3) and (if x1_v3 <= x2_v3 and x1_v3 <= x3_v3 then x1_v3 else if x2_v3 <= x1_v3 and x2_v3 <= x3_v3 then x2_v3 else x3_v3) <= 100;
  assert (if x1_v3 >= x2_v3 and x1_v3 >= x3_v3 then x1_v3 else if x2_v3 >= x1_v3 and x2_v3 >= x3_v3 then x2_v3 else x3_v3) != (if x1_v3 <= x2_v3 and x1_v3 <= x3_v3 then x1_v3 else if x2_v3 <= x1_v3 and x2_v3 <= x3_v3 then x2_v3 else x3_v3);
  assert (if x1_v3 >= x2_v3 and x1_v3 >= x3_v3 then x1_v3 else if x2_v3 >= x1_v3 and x2_v3 >= x3_v3 then x2_v3 else x3_v3) > (if x1_v3 <= x2_v3 and x1_v3 <= x3_v3 then x1_v3 else if x2_v3 <= x1_v3 and x2_v3 <= x3_v3 then x2_v3 else x3_v3);
  assert (if x1_v3 >= x2_v3 and x1_v3 >= x3_v3 then x1_v3 else if x2_v3 >= x1_v3 and x2_v3 >= x3_v3 then x2_v3 else x3_v3) - (if x1_v3 <= x2_v3 and x1_v3 <= x3_v3 then x1_v3 else if x2_v3 <= x1_v3 and x2_v3 <= x3_v3 then x2_v3 else x3_v3) >= 1;
  assert (if x1_v3 >= x2_v3 and x1_v3 >= x3_v3 then x1_v3 else if x2_v3 >= x1_v3 and x2_v3 >= x3_v3 then x2_v3 else x3_v3) - (if x1_v3 <= x2_v3 and x1_v3 <= x3_v3 then x1_v3 else if x2_v3 <= x1_v3 and x2_v3 <= x3_v3 then x2_v3 else x3_v3) <= 99;

{
  var max_pos: int := if x1 >= x2 and x1 >= x3 then x1 else if x2 >= x1 and x2 >= x3 then x2 else x3;
  var min_pos: int := if x1 <= x2 and x1 <= x3 then x1 else if x2 <= x1 and x2 <= x3 then x2 else x3;
  result := max_pos - min_pos;

Problem: Given a list of university entrance years for groups that student Igor joined, determine Igor's university entrance year. Igor joins his own group and all groups where the entrance year differs by at most x years from his entrance year. The solution computes Igor's entrance year as the average of all group years.
Signature: vericoding_da0101__solve(int, seq) -> int
t 1
gate recursion
task vericoding_da0101__solve(n: int, years: seq) returns (result: int)
  requires validInput(n, years)
  ensures result == sumRange(years, 0, len(years)) / n
spec fun validInput(n_v: int, years_v: seq): bool
  decreases 0
= n_v > 0 and len(years_v) == n_v
spec fun sumRange(s: seq, start: int, end: int): int
  decreases end - start
= if 0 <= start and start <= end and end <= len(s) then if start == end then 0 else s[start] + sumRange(s, start + 1, end) else 0
lemma sumRangeAppend(s_v: seq, start_v: int, end_v: int)
  requires 0 <= start_v
  requires start_v <= end_v
  requires end_v < len(s_v)
  ensures sumRange(s_v, start_v, end_v + 1) == sumRange(s_v, start_v, end_v) + s_v[end_v]
  decreases end_v - start_v
{
  if start_v == end_v {
    assert sumRange(s_v, start_v, end_v) == 0;
    assert sumRange(s_v, start_v, end_v + 1) == s_v[start_v];
    assert s_v[start_v] == s_v[end_v];
  } else {
    assert sumRange(s_v, start_v, end_v + 1) == s_v[start_v] + sumRange(s_v, start_v + 1, end_v + 1);
    sumRangeAppend(s_v, start_v + 1, end_v);
    assert sumRange(s_v, start_v + 1, end_v + 1) == sumRange(s_v, start_v + 1, end_v) + s_v[end_v];
    assert sumRange(s_v, start_v, end_v) == s_v[start_v] + sumRange(s_v, start_v + 1, end_v);
    assert sumRange(s_v, start_v, end_v + 1) == sumRange(s_v, start_v, end_v) + s_v[end_v];
  }
}
{
  var sum: int := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant 0 <= i and i <= n
    invariant n == len(years)
    invariant sum == sumRange(years, 0, i)
    decreases n - i
  {
    sumRangeAppend(years, 0, i);
    sum := sum + years[i];
    i := i + 1;
  }
  result := sum / n;
}


Problem: Given r red balloons, g green balloons, and b blue balloons, determine the maximum number of tables that can be decorated. Each table requires exactly 3 balloons, and no table can have all 3 balloons of the same color.
Signature: vericoding_da0110__solve(int, int, int) -> int
t 1
gate recursion
task vericoding_da0110__solve(r: int, g: int, b: int) returns (result: int)
  requires validInput(r, g, b)
  ensures result == maxTables(r, g, b)
  ensures result >= 0
spec fun min(a: int, b_v: int): int
  decreases 0
= if a <= b_v then a else b_v
spec fun validInput(r_v: int, g_v: int, b_v2: int): bool
  decreases 0
= r_v >= 0 and g_v >= 0 and b_v2 >= 0
spec fun maxTables(r_v2: int, g_v2: int, b_v3: int): int
  decreases 0
= if validInput(r_v2, g_v2, b_v3) then min(min(min((r_v2 + g_v2 + b_v3) / 3, r_v2 + g_v2), r_v2 + b_v3), g_v2 + b_v3) else 0
lemma maxTablesNonNegative(r_v3: int, g_v3: int, b_v4: int)
  requires validInput(r_v3, g_v3, b_v4)
  ensures maxTables(r_v3, g_v3, b_v4) >= 0
{
}
{
  var sum_all: int := (r + g + b) / 3;
  var sum_rg: int := r + g;
  var sum_rb: int := r + b;
  var sum_gb: int := g + b;
  var min1: int := min(sum_all, sum_rg);
  var min2: int := min(min1, sum_rb);
  result := min(min2, sum_gb);
  maxTablesNonNegative(r, g, b);
}


Problem: Given a positive integer n, calculate the value of the alternating sum: f(n) = -1 + 2 - 3 + 4 - 5 + ... + (-1)^n × n
Signature: vericoding_da0123__solve(int) -> int
t 1
gate recursion
task vericoding_da0123__solve(n: int) returns (result: int)
  requires validInput(n)
  ensures result == alternatingSum(n)
  ensures n % 2 == 0 ==> result == n / 2
  ensures n % 2 != 0 ==> result == n / 2 - n
spec fun validInput(n_v: int): bool
  decreases 0
= n_v > 0
spec fun alternatingSum(n_v2: int): int
  decreases n_v2
= if n_v2 > 0 then if n_v2 == 1 then -(1) else alternatingSum(n_v2 - 1) + (if n_v2 % 2 == 0 then n_v2 else -n_v2) else 0
lemma alternatingSum_formula(n_v3: int)
  requires n_v3 > 0
  ensures alternatingSum(n_v3) == (if n_v3 % 2 == 0 then n_v3 / 2 else n_v3 / 2 - n_v3)
  decreases n_v3
{
  if n_v3 == 1 {
    assert alternatingSum(1) == -(1);
    assert 1 / 2 - 1 == -(1);
    assert alternatingSum(1) == 1 / 2 - 1;
  } else {
    alternatingSum_formula(n_v3 - 1);
    if n_v3 % 2 == 0 {
      assert alternatingSum(n_v3) == alternatingSum(n_v3 - 1) + n_v3;
      assert alternatingSum(n_v3 - 1) == (n_v3 - 1) / 2 - (n_v3 - 1);
      assert alternatingSum(n_v3) == (n_v3 - 1) / 2 - (n_v3 - 1) + n_v3;
      assert (n_v3 - 1) / 2 - (n_v3 - 1) + n_v3 == (n_v3 - 1) / 2 + 1;
      assert n_v3 == 2 * (n_v3 / 2);
      assert (n_v3 - 1) / 2 == n_v3 / 2 - 1;
      assert (n_v3 - 1) / 2 + 1 == n_v3 / 2;
      assert alternatingSum(n_v3) == n_v3 / 2;
      assert alternatingSum(n_v3) == n_v3 / 2;
    } else {
      assert alternatingSum(n_v3) == alternatingSum(n_v3 - 1) - n_v3;
      assert alternatingSum(n_v3 - 1) == (n_v3 - 1) / 2;
      assert alternatingSum(n_v3) == (n_v3 - 1) / 2 - n_v3;
      assert n_v3 == 2 * (n_v3 / 2) + 1;
      assert (n_v3 - 1) / 2 == n_v3 / 2;
      assert (n_v3 - 1) / 2 == n_v3 / 2;
      assert alternatingSum(n_v3) == n_v3 / 2 - n_v3;
    }
  }
}
{
  var i: int := 1;
  result := -(1);
  while i < n
    invariant 1 <= i and i <= n
    invariant result == alternatingSum(i)
    decreases n - i
  {
    i := i + 1;
    if i % 2 == 0 {
      result := result + i;
    } else {
      result := result - i;
    }
  }
  alternatingSum_formula(n);
}

    result := max_val - sum_of_other_two + 1;

Problem: /* Cumulative Sums over Arrays */ /* Daniel Cavalheiro 57869 Pedro Nunes 57854 */ //(a) //(b) //(c)
Signature: vericoding_dd0040__query(seq, int, int) -> int
t 1
gate recursion
task vericoding_dd0040__query(a: seq, i: int, j: int) returns (res: int)
  requires 0 <= i
  requires i <= j
  requires j <= len(a)
  ensures res == sum(a, i, j)
spec fun sum(a_v: seq, i_v: int, j_v: int): int
  decreases j_v - i_v
= if 0 <= i_v and i_v <= j_v and j_v <= len(a_v) then if i_v == j_v then 0 else a_v[i_v] + sum(a_v, i_v + 1, j_v) else 0
lemma sum_extend(a_v2: seq, i_v2: int, j_v2: int)
  requires 0 <= i_v2
  requires i_v2 <= j_v2
  requires j_v2 < len(a_v2)
  ensures sum(a_v2, i_v2, j_v2) + a_v2[j_v2] == sum(a_v2, i_v2, j_v2 + 1)
  decreases j_v2 - i_v2
{
  if i_v2 == j_v2 {
    assert sum(a_v2, i_v2, j_v2) == 0;
    assert sum(a_v2, i_v2, j_v2 + 1) == a_v2[i_v2] + sum(a_v2, i_v2 + 1, j_v2 + 1) and a_v2[i_v2] + sum(a_v2, i_v2 + 1, j_v2 + 1) == a_v2[i_v2] + 0 and a_v2[i_v2] + 0 == a_v2[i_v2];
  } else {
    assert sum(a_v2, i_v2, j_v2 + 1) == a_v2[i_v2] + sum(a_v2, i_v2 + 1, j_v2 + 1);
    sum_extend(a_v2, i_v2 + 1, j_v2);
    assert sum(a_v2, i_v2 + 1, j_v2) + a_v2[j_v2] == sum(a_v2, i_v2 + 1, j_v2 + 1);
    assert sum(a_v2, i_v2, j_v2) == a_v2[i_v2] + sum(a_v2, i_v2 + 1, j_v2);
  }
}
{
  var s: int := 0;
  var idx: int := i;
  while idx < j
    invariant i <= idx and idx <= j
    invariant s == sum(a, i, idx)
    decreases j - idx
  {
    s := s + a[idx];
    sum_extend(a, i, idx);
    idx := idx + 1;
  }
  res := s;
}

  requires forall k in [0, len(s)) . s[k] >= 0 and s[k] <= 1114111
  requires forall i in [0, len(s)) . 97 <= s[i] and s[i] <= 122
  ensures len(s) == len(t_v)
  ensures forall i_v in [0, len(s)) . t_v[i_v] == decode_char(s[i_v])

  decreases n

task has_duplicate(s: seq) returns (r: bool)
  ensures r ==> (exists i in [0, len(s)) . exists j in [0, len(s)) . i < j and s[i] == s[j])
  ensures not r ==> (forall i in [0, len(s)) . forall j in [0, len(s)) . i < j ==> s[i] != s[j])
{
  r := false;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s) and not r
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= len(s)
    invariant not r ==> (forall a in [0, i) . forall b in [0, len(s)) . a < b ==> s[a] != s[b])
    invariant r ==> (exists a in [0, len(s)) . exists b in [0, len(s)) . a < b and s[a] == s[b])
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    var j: int := i + 1;
    while j < len(s) and not r
      invariant i >= 0 and i < len(s)
      invariant j >= i + 1 and j <= len(s)
      invariant not r ==> (forall b in [i + 1, j) . b < len(s) ==> s[i] != s[b])
      invariant r ==> (exists a in [0, len(s)) . exists b in [0, len(s)) . a < b and s[a] == s[b])
      decreases len(s) - j
    {
      if s[i] == s[j] {
        r := true;
      } else {
      }
      j := j + 1;
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}


t 1
gate loops
task is_prime(n: int) returns (r: bool)
  requires n >= 2
  ensures r == (forall d in [2, n) . n % d != 0)
{
  var d: int := 2;
  while d < n
    invariant d >= 2 and d <= n
    invariant forall k in [2, d) . n % k != 0
    decreases n - d
  {
    if n % d == 0 {
      return false;
    } else {
    }
    d := d + 1;
  }
  r := true;
}


t 1
gate loops
task linear_search(s: seq, x: int) returns (r: int)
  ensures r == -1 or r >= 0 and r < len(s) and s[r] == x
  ensures r == -1 ==> (forall i in [0, len(s)) . s[i] != x)
  ensures r != -1 ==> (forall i in [0, r) . i < len(s) ==> s[i] != x)
{
  r := -1;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s) and r == -1
    invariant r == -1 ==> (forall j in [0, i) . j < len(s) ==> s[j] != x)
    invariant r != -1 ==> r >= 0 and r < len(s) and s[r] == x and (forall j in [0, r) . j < len(s) ==> s[j] != x)
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= len(s)
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] == x {
      r := i;
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}


t 0
task max(x: int, y: int) returns (r: int)
  ensures r >= x
  ensures r >= y
  ensures r == x or r == y
{
  if x >= y {
    r := x;
  } else {
    r := y;
  }
}


t 1
gate loops
task min_max(s: seq) returns (r: (int, int))
  requires len(s) > 0
  ensures forall k in [0, len(s)) . r.0 <= s[k]
  ensures forall k in [0, len(s)) . s[k] <= r.1
  ensures exists k in [0, len(s)) . r.0 == s[k]
  ensures exists k in [0, len(s)) . r.1 == s[k]
{
  var lo: int := s[0];
  var hi: int := s[0];
  var i: int := 1;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 1 <= i
    invariant i <= len(s)
    invariant forall k in [0, i) . lo <= s[k]
    invariant forall k in [0, i) . s[k] <= hi
    invariant exists k in [0, i) . lo == s[k]
    invariant exists k in [0, i) . hi == s[k]
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] < lo {
      lo := s[i];
    } else {
    }
    if s[i] > hi {
      hi := s[i];
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
  r := (lo, hi);
}

  ensures r == function or r == method

  if predicate >= method {
    r := predicate;
  } else {
    r := method;
  }
}

t 1
gate loops
task reverse(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  ensures len(r) == len(s)
  ensures forall k in [0, len(s)) . r[k] == s[len(s) - 1 - k]
{
  r := seq(len(s), 0);
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant 0 <= i and i <= len(s)
    invariant len(r) == len(s)
    invariant forall k in [0, i) . r[k] == s[len(s) - 1 - k]
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    r := r[i := s[len(s) - 1 - i]];
    i := i + 1;
  }
}


t 1
gate loops
task row_max_len(m: seq<seq>) returns (r: int)
  requires len(m) > 0
  ensures forall k in [0, len(m)) . len(m[k]) <= r
  ensures exists k in [0, len(m)) . len(m[k]) == r
{
  r := len(m[0]);
  var i: int := 1;
  while i < len(m)
    invariant forall j in [0, i) . j < len(m) ==> len(m[j]) <= r
    invariant exists j in [0, i) . j < len(m) and len(m[j]) == r
    invariant i >= 1
    decreases len(m) - i
  {
    if len(m[i]) > r {
      r := len(m[i]);
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}

gate quantifiers
task seq_max(s: seq) returns (r: int)
  requires len(s) > 0
  ensures forall i in [0, len(s)) . r >= s[i]
  ensures exists i in [0, len(s)) . r == s[i]
{
  r := s[0];
  var i: int := 1;
  while i < len(s)
    invariant forall j in [0, i) . j < len(s) ==> r >= s[j]
    invariant exists j in [0, i) . j < len(s) and r == s[j]
    invariant i >= 1
    decreases len(s) - i
  {
    if s[i] > r {
      r := s[i];
    } else {
    }
    i := i + 1;
  }
}


t 1
gate loops
task sum_upto(n: int) returns (r: int)
  requires n >= 0
  ensures 2 * r == n * (n + 1)
{
  r := 0;
  var i: int := 0;
  while i < n
    invariant 2 * r == i * (i + 1)
    invariant i >= 0 and i <= n
    decreases n - i
  {
    i := i + 1;
    r := r + i;
  }
}


t 1
gate quantifiers
task swap(s: seq, i: int, j: int) returns (r: seq)
  requires 0 <= i and i < len(s)
  requires 0 <= j and j < len(s)
  ensures len(r) == len(s)
  ensures r[i] == s[j]
  ensures r[j] == s[i]
  ensures forall k in [0, len(s)) . k != i and k != j ==> r[k] == s[k]
{
  var tmp: int := s[i];
  r := s[i := s[j]];
  r := r[j := tmp];
}


t 1
gate loops
task tail(s: seq) returns (r: seq)
  requires len(s) > 0
  ensures len(r) == len(s) - 1
  ensures forall k in [0, len(r)) . r[k] == s[k + 1]
{
  r := s[1..len(s)];
}


t 1
task word_count(s: seq) returns (r: int)
  ensures r == len(s.split())
{
  var t2: seq := s[0..len(s)];
  r := len(t2.split());
}
